- **coeus**: Coeus is a systematic debugger that identifies, analyzes, and resolves bugs through methodical investigation — it reproduces issues, performs root cause analysis, implements minimal targeted fixes, and verifies with tests. Use Coeus when you need to diagnose and fix bugs, test failures, or unexpected behavior in existing code, especially when the issue requires careful investigation rather than new feature development. ([xcillik/agents](https://github.com/xcillik/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/coeus.md))

- **coffee-coach**: Home coffee coach grounded in the optional knowledge sources the user configured in profile/coaches.md (e.g. YouTube transcripts of baristas and roasters), the manuals of the user's coffee gear and the user's own notes. - **coffee-developer**: Пишет код строго по техническому заданию. Звать, когда нужно написать код по заданию или реализовать уже описанные пункты. Не переписывает задание. ([coffeebreak-ai/coffee-agents](https://github.com/coffeebreak-ai/coffee-agents/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/coffee-developer.md) · ★2)
- **coffee-intelligence**: Asistente analitico que examina templates HTML/wireframes, los entiende como dominios de negocio y propone una base de datos completa siguiendo las reglas de db-rules.md (CoffeeSoft/Huubie). Opcional: se conecta a MCP MySQL para inspeccionar el esquema vivo. ([CoffeeSoftDev/huubie](https://github.com/CoffeeSoftDev/huubie/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/coffee-intelligence.md) · 2 copies)

- **coffee-qa**: Проверяет изменения на соответствие техническому заданию. Звать, когда нужно проверить, найти ошибки или сверить код с заданием. Не чинит код. ([coffeebreak-ai/coffee-agents](https://github.com/coffeebreak-ai/coffee-agents/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/coffee-qa.md) · ★2)

- **cofounder**: Business thinking partner that challenges assumptions instead of validating them. Use for business model, go-to-market, pricing, prioritization and hiring calls, AI/automation strategy, and agent-system design framed as a business decision (blast radius, human-in-the-loop, unit economics) - and when the user is juggling several big decisions and needs to separate what is urgent from what feels urgent. For technology selection, build-vs-buy and system architecture, use cto-advisor. ([samhvw8/dotfiles](https://github.com/samhvw8/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/.ccp/hub/agents/cofounder.md) · ★15)
- **cofounder**: Use when the founder asks what's going on with their company, what to do next, or wants a status check-in on billing, setup, CRM, or the roadmap. Not for engineering questions about the Cofounder platform itself. ([General-Intelligence-Company/cofounder-plugin](https://github.com/General-Intelligence-Company/cofounder-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/cofounder/agents/cofounder.md) · ★1)
- **cofounder**: Critical-thinking business co-founder that enforces prioritization, challenges assumptions, and becomes smarter over time. Spawned by the /cofounder orchestrator skill. ([rvanbaalen/skills](https://github.com/rvanbaalen/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/cofounder/agents/cofounder.md))
- **cofounder-agent**: Strategy-layer assistant for a program's cofounder. Helps author and audit the six v1 strategy registries (pitch decks, VPCs, business plans, GTM, financial projections, market analyses) in &lt;program&gt;-strategy. Read-only on business-layer artifacts (outcomes, flows, risks, vocabulary); read-write on strategy-layer staging only. All writes require HITL confirm — the cofounder retains full authority over every artifact. ([inprimex/otaman-plugin](https://github.com/inprimex/otaman-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/cofounder-agent.md) · ★3)
- **cofounder-agent**: Stage 0-4 specialist — co-founder search, vetting, equity splits, relationship management, conflict resolution, and first hire decisions. ([EaseStart/easestart-public](https://github.com/EaseStart/easestart-public/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/cofounder-agent.md))
- **cofounder-coach**: **Version:** 1.0 · **Last reviewed:** 2026-05-07 · **Owner:** People Pod ([dahaliztupe-sketch/Kalmeron-Two](https://github.com/dahaliztupe-sketch/Kalmeron-Two/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cofounder-coach.md) · ★1)
- **cofounder-reviewer**: Use this agent to review a diff or PR against the repo's CoFounder VibeSpecs. Checks for secrets, mock data in prod, scope violations, design-system breaches, missing tests, and deviations from referenced specs. Returns a structured review with confidence-filtered findings — reports only high-priority issues that actually matter. Use proactively after a feature implementation or before opening a PR. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-reviewer.md))
- **cofounder-scout**: Spawnable sub-agent variant of the pattern-scout skill. Delegate to this agent when you want a separate, isolated context to find existing patterns in the repo before writing new code — searches for similar functions, components, hooks, models, and routes, then returns ranked matches with extend-vs-create recommendations. Use proactively when the user asks to "add", "build", "create", or "extract" something non-trivial. Distinct from the `cofounder-pattern-scout` skill, which is auto-invoked rather than spawned. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-scout.md))
- **cofounder-spec-writer**: Use this agent when the user describes a feature idea but the scope, acceptance criteria, or data sources aren't clear. Asks 3–5 clarifying questions, searches the repo for prior art, and writes a VibeSpec-compliant feature spec to `specs/&lt;slug&gt;.spec.yml`. Use proactively before any non-trivial feature implementation. ([waymaker-ai/ranavibe](https://github.com/waymaker-ai/ranavibe/blob/HEAD/packages/claude-code-plugin/agents/cofounder-spec-writer.md))
- **cog-a11y**: Cognitive accessibility reviewer for ADRs before ratification. ([windyroad/agent-plugins](https://github.com/windyroad/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/packages/architect/agents/cog-a11y.md) · ★6)
- **cog-architect**: Designs a new cog from a brief. Asks clarifying questions about sensor source, output shape, latency budget, then produces Cargo.toml, cog.toml, and src/main.rs that conform to ADR-091 (cog-sensor-sources) and the cog-development skill. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-architect.md) · ★3)
- **cog-deployer**: Cross-compile + GCS upload + registry bump for a cog. Confirms the rollout by reinstalling on a connected seed via MCP. References the cog-deployment skill for the order of operations. - **cog-ops**: Day-2 operations for cogs in the field. Triages logs, checks health, runs OTA, drives rollback. Talks to the seed exclusively over MCP — no SSH. References the cog-operations skill. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-ops.md) · ★3)
- **cog-specialist**: Mechanical gear math specialist for CAD-ready measurements, ratios, and gear geometry across spur, helical, bevel, and worm gear types. ([JamesNapoletano/ripperdoc](https://github.com/JamesNapoletano/ripperdoc/blob/HEAD/agents/cog-specialist.md))
- **cog-tester**: Runs the test loop for a cog: cargo check, ARM cross-compile, install on a seed via MCP, smoke-test a few output frames, stop. Reports each step's outcome. ([cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cognitum-one/cognitum-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/cog-dev/agents/cog-tester.md) · ★3)
- **cogitator**: Sonnet cruncher-lever warden. Diffs the DSL→cruncher buff extraction before/after a re-authoring pass so cosine-motivated re-phrasing cannot silently drop stackable-buff levers. Use for "check the cruncher levers survived this re-author", "diff coverage for &lt;faction&gt; against the previous state". Prompt must include the touched ability_ids/factions and a before-state reference (committed baseline or a saved coverage snapshot). Returns a single JSON object as final message. ([wn-mitch/40kdc-data](https://github.com/wn-mitch/40kdc-data/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/cogitator.md) · ★39)
- **cogllama**: Synthesizes OpenCog cognitive components into pure C/C++ implementations using llama.cpp and GGML as computational substrate. Implements the complete OpenCog cognitive architecture as high-performance tensor operations for AGI development. ([OzCog/opencog-unified](https://github.com/OzCog/opencog-unified/blob/HEAD/hypermind/.github/agents/cogllama.md) · ★3 · 10 copies)
- **cogmo-reviewer**: Read-only conformance reviewer for cogmo. Checks a PR, branch or diff range (`code &lt;target&gt;`) against the repo's rules, idioms, architecture, state-machine, Inngest, data, test and bookkeeping conventions, or a design doc (`design &lt;path&gt;`) against state-machines.md "Design before code". - **cold-reviewer**: Zero-context code reviewer. Reviews changes cold - no spec, no project knowledge, no domain context. Catches what familiarity blinds you to. Delegate when you want a fresh-eyes pass before merging, or as part of a multi-perspective review. ([krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso](https://github.com/krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★27)
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold diff review by ref. Resolves a commit SHA or base branch without intent framing, cites every finding as severity/claim/file:line, writes an incremental report, and never edits source. Use after gates on a behavior-bearing diff. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **cold-reviewer**: ../../agents/cold-reviewer.md ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Reads this branch's changes against the pull request's base cold, the way Codex and Copilot will, and reports what they would raise. Use before the first review request of a pull request and after each round's fixes. Read-only by instruction; it posts nothing. ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Performs cold-context single-plan critique when no separate requirements/spec file is available. Infers the problem from the plan itself and surfaces unchallenged assumptions, alternatives, and load-bearing bets. Use only when /review-plan determines no spec file exists. ([DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact](https://github.com/DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cold-reviewer**: Pre-PR code-review gate for this CLI monorepo. Reviews a branch against its GitHub issue with fresh, adversarial eyes — given only the issue number and the branch name, no conversation context. Run at the end of every plan that adds a CLI crate or a command (new-cli-crate / add-cli-command), before opening the PR. ([lucabro81/CLI-monorepo](https://github.com/lucabro81/CLI-monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Pre-merge code-review gate. Reviews a branch against its issue with fresh, adversarial eyes — given only the issue, the branch and the review scope, no conversation context. Use before merging any PR. ([lucabro81/mercury-fw](https://github.com/lucabro81/mercury-fw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Blind cold reviewer for the kit's gate ladders — a fresh, no-build-context adversarial review of an artifact against its contract. Invoke with ONLY the payload named in core/REVIEW.md (the change + the redacted contract + invariants + claimed tier); never the build conversation or the author's advocacy. ([Madman131/workflow-kit](https://github.com/Madman131/workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a pull request for /work-ticket, round 1 and the verify rounds after it. Fresh context, refutes by default, reports findings and edits nothing. ([matldupont/slipway](https://github.com/matldupont/slipway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Review one generated Taku workspace using only local evidence ([Taku-OS/taku-publisher](https://github.com/Taku-OS/taku-publisher/blob/HEAD/packages/repo-to-stax-converter/benchmark/agents/kimi-cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a PR or commit range it did not write, and re-review of the fix commits when resumed. Reports findings; never edits, commits or comments. ([tarka1939/My_Site](https://github.com/tarka1939/My_Site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))

- **cold-reviewer-strongest**: Cold review of a pull request for /work-ticket when a review named a risky surface (round 1 or a verify) or the diff changes a file the run is judged by, a gate file among them. - **cold-scorer**: Adversarial cold-context evaluator. Spawn ONLY with (a) the diff or output artifact, (b) the manifest of what was claimed done, (c) test/lint/exit-code artifacts. Never give it the parent's conversation, reasoning, or excuses. Its output is the only valid source for ledger failure entries and skill-quality scores. ([ktg-one/AiA](https://github.com/ktg-one/AiA/blob/HEAD/sia-loop/agents/cold-scorer.md))
- **cold-solver**: Blind checker for Economics Academy content. Give it ONE temp file path and a mode - "solve" (stems + options, no answers: returns a letter and one-line reasoning per question), "verify" (flashcards + the notes page each must match: returns AGREE/DISAGREE per card), or "tag" (past-paper questions + a taxonomy excerpt: returns topic slugs per question). It never sees the answer key, the source data or a review sheet, so its result is an independent second opinion. ([eliotking01/economics-academy](https://github.com/eliotking01/economics-academy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-solver.md))
- **cold-start-auditor**: Run this agent periodically, or after any significant debugging session, to verify that the repo's knowledge record is lossless. It simulates an agent with zero conversational context — only the repo — and attempts to answer the questions a future maintainer would need answered. Failures identify exactly which knowledge is trapped in conversations or memory rather than in the durable record. ([clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor](https://github.com/clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-start-auditor.md))
- **cold-start-author**: Usability test of austere-axi's agent-facing docs. Acts as an agent that has never seen the project, writes a review page for a given scenario from `--help` and `spec` alone, and reports where the docs failed it. Launch with a scenario that needs the changed component (without naming it), the repo path, and an output path outside the repo. ([MandalorianBatman/austere-axi](https://github.com/MandalorianBatman/austere-axi/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/cold-start-author.md) · ★8)
- **cold-start-beta-tester**: A working designer using Creative Companion for the first time with zero knowledge of how it was built — takes a client brief (usually from new-client-persona), tries to actually produce the deliverable through the platform, and reports every hole that stopped them. Drives the running app only; never reads the repo, the docs, or the source. Use to find what the platform is missing, as opposed to what is broken. ([nicholmahania-spec/creative-companion](https://github.com/nicholmahania-spec/creative-companion/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-start-beta-tester.md))

- **cold-start-tester**: Fill the placeholders and pass the text below as the agent prompt. Write `{{SKILL_SUMMARY}}` in your own words; never quote the skill.
